The resulting capacity-aware target is optimized with full-vocabulary reverse KL on student trajectories, retaining the standard OPD framework. In 8B-to-2B distillation, FP-OPD improves all seven evaluated multimodal benchmarks. It raises the average score by 2.77 points over the pretrained student and by 1.60 points over standard OPD. These results demonstrate that locally realizable teacher corrections provide a more effective target for distilling compact vision--language models.

</details>


### [168] [Riemannian Attention Mechanisms for Transformers: A Theoretical Framework and Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01283)
*Sen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Transformer中欧几里得内积注意力导致表示秩随深度双重指数衰减的结构性缺陷，提出用学到的逐令牌黎曼度量替代平坦欧几里得度量，并给出了Fiber Bundle Transformer的完整架构规范。理论贡献包括证明异质黎曼注意力分数非Gram矩阵、低秩度量因子的高效计算可行性，以及明确的核心开放问题：异质黎曼度量能否防止秩坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dong等人（2021）已证明纯自注意力堆栈中，基于欧几里得内积的注意力计算会导致表示秩随深度双重指数衰减，这是Transformer架构的固有结构性局限。作者希望从数学层面（而非通过工程技巧）解决这一秩坍缩问题。

Method: (1) 理论上证明具有异质逐令牌度量的黎曼注意力分数是非Gram矩阵，无法以O(d)维因子分解为QK^T；(2) 利用低秩度量因子将测地距离计算降至O(d*r)、度量求逆降至O(d*r^2)（通过Woodbury恒等式），远低于一般矩阵O(d^3)的开销；(3) 提出Fiber Bundle Transformer完整架构：每个令牌位置携带自己的黎曼度量、注意力为测地距离计算、前馈更新使用度量预条件步、连接携带显式曲率和挠率代理。

Result: 论文完成了三个方面的理论贡献：证明了异质黎曼注意力分数的非Gram结构性质；建立了低秩度量因子下所有几何操作的高效计算复杂度；给出了Fiber Bundle Transformer的完整架构规范，并推导出关于正确实现的几何架构的形式化预测。该论文定位为理论分析和架构设计，尚未进行实证验证。

Conclusion: 论文明确指出核心开放问题：证明或证伪异质黎曼度量能否防止行随机注意力矩阵引起的秩坍缩。作者强调这是结构性观察而非秩保持的证明，实证验证留给未来工作。

Abstract: All Transformer-based large language models compute attention via the Euclidean inner product, an architectural choice that Dong et al. (2021) proved causes representational rank to decay doubly exponentially with depth in pure self-attention stacks. We develop a theoretical framework that targets this structural limitation at the mathematical level by replacing the flat Euclidean metric with learned per-token Riemannian metrics. Our contributions are threefold. (1) We prove that Riemannian attention scores with heterogeneous per-token metrics are non-Gram---they cannot be factorized as QK^T with factorization dimension O(d). We are explicit that this is a structural observation, not a proof of rank preservation. (2) We establish that low-rank metric factors render all geometric operations tractable: geodesic distance in O(d*r) per token and metric inversion in O(d*r^2) via the Woodbury identity---both far below the O(d^3) cost of a general matrix---making Riemannian attention feasible at billion-parameter scale with negligible overhead. (3) We present the Fiber Bundle Transformer, a complete architecture specification in which each token position carries its own Riemannian metric, attention is geodesic distance computation, feed-forward updates use metric-preconditioned steps, and the connection carries explicit curvature and torsion proxies. We derive formal predictions about correctly implemented geometric architectures and identify the central open problem: proving or disproving that heterogeneous Riemannian metrics prevent the rank collapse that row-stochastic attention matrices otherwise cause. This paper presents theoretical analysis and architectural design; empirical validation is the subject of future work.

</details>


### [169] [Training nGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01284)
*Ilya Loshchilov,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对归一化Transformer（nGPT）的实用训练配方，将其应用于现代混合Mamba-2-Transformer混合专家（MoE）模型，实现了与未归一化模型相同的验证损失但仅需约一半的训练token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: nGPT通过将参数向量和激活向量约束在单位超球面上实现超球面表示学习，但缺乏实用的训练配方，特别是在现代混合MoE架构上的应用尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 训练配方包括：Logit梯度预处理、对数学习率衰减、GatedAdamW优化器、角度更新控制以及可选的探索机制。这些组件协同工作以支持nGPT在混合Mamba-2-Transformer MoE模型上的高效训练。

Result: 在14B总参数的模型规模下，nGPT模型相比使用AdamW训练的未归一化同架构混合MoE模型，达到相同验证损失所需的训练token数量减少约一半。该配方在至多14B总参数的模型范围内具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的训练配方有效地提升了nGPT的训练效率，使其在混合MoE架构上展现显著的token效率优势，且具有良好的规模扩展性。

Abstract: The normalized Transformer (nGPT) realizes hyperspherical representation learning by constraining model parameter vectors and activation vectors to the unit hypersphere. In this paper, we describe a practical training recipe for nGPT and evaluate it on modern hybrid Mamba-2--Transformer Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. The recipe introduces Logit Gradient Preconditioning, Logarithmic Learning Rate Decay, GatedAdamW, angular update control, and optional exploration mechanisms. Compared with an unnormalized model of the same hybrid MoE architecture trained with AdamW, the 14B-total-parameter nGPT model reaches the same validation loss using approximately half as many training tokens. The recipe scales across the models considered, which contain up to 14B total parameters.

</details>


### [170] [Stop When Memory Suffices: Evidence-Conditioned Progressive Execution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01285)
*Yidan Lin,Kaixiang Wang,Jiong Lou,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Router-Mem是一个面向长时程智能体记忆的证据条件化渐进执行框架，通过轻量级充分性路由器在推理时进行单标记决策，在保证答案质量的同时显著降低推理延迟。在AMA-Bench和BEAM上分别取得55.17%和38.77%的分数，同时平均推理时间分别降低27.3%和25.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统存在两难：结构化压缩历史虽然在线成本低，但可能丢失时间、因果或跨步骤依赖；深度研究虽提升证据覆盖但带来高延迟和推理成本。作者希望探索能否在保持低在线延迟的同时实现强答案质量。

Method: Router-Mem包含三个核心组件：1) 共享低成本的检索前缀获取证据；2) 轻量级充分性路由器预测上下文是否支持提前终止，通过证据级监督和理由条件化表示蒸馏训练，实现推理时的单标记决策；3) 当证据不足时，复用检索命中结果扩展记忆块并进行更深层次的分析与聚合，形成渐进式执行。

Result: 在AMA-Bench和BEAM基准上，Router-Mem分别取得55.17%和38.77%的分数，相比完整记忆执行，平均推理时间分别减少27.3%和25.5%。

Conclusion: Router-Mem证明了记忆系统可以在保持低在线延迟的同时获得强答案质量，通过证据条件化的渐进执行策略，在效率与质量之间实现了有效平衡。

Abstract: The continued development of LLMs toward persistent and adaptive intelligence increasingly requires long-term memory mechanisms that preserve and reuse information across interactions. Existing memory systems either compress and structure histories for efficient access or perform deep research over broader trajectories. The former lowers online cost but may omit temporal, causal, or cross-step dependencies, while the latter improves evidence coverage at substantial latency and inference cost. This raises a key question: can a memory system achieve strong answer quality while maintaining low online latency? We introduce Router-Mem, an evidence-conditioned progressive execution framework for long-horizon agent memory. Router-Mem first applies a shared low-cost retrieval prefix to obtain evidence. A lightweight sufficiency router then predicts whether the context supports early termination, which enable a single-token decision at inference time. It is trained with evidence-level supervision and rationale-conditioned representation distillation. When evidence is insufficient, Router-Mem reuses retrieval hits to expand memory blocks and perform deeper analysis and aggregation. Experiments on AMA-Bench and BEAM show that Router-Mem achieves 55.17\% and 38.77\% score while reducing average inference time by 27.3\% and 25.5\% compared with full memory execution.

</details>


### [171] [FedChronos: Federated Fine-Tuning of Time-Series Foundation Models for Privacy-Preserving Commodity Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01290)
*Amit Sharma,Nitin Auluck,Akramul Azim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedChronos框架，将预训练时间序列基础模型（TSFM）Chronos通过联邦参数高效微调（LoRA）适配到数据无法集中的分布式场景中，解决了现有联邦时间序列工作未覆盖"固定骨干+适配器"的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型已展现强大预测能力，但在机构数据碎片化（受监管、竞争或主权约束无法集中数据）场景下的适配问题尚未被探索。现有的联邦时间序列方法要么从头预训练，要么对齐原型，均未解决对已预训练模型的固定骨干适配问题。

Method: 对Chronos-T5骨干网络应用低秩适配（LoRA），在分布式客户端上使用FedAvg和FedProx进行联邦训练，仅传输轻量级适配器权重（每轮384KB，较全模型交换减少86倍）；实验在印度15个农产品市场、9个邦的日度商品价格数据上评估，并引入差分隐私（DP）噪声作为隐式正则化。

Result: 天真的LoRA微调在小客户端数据集上严重过拟合，性能甚至低于零样本。加入DP噪声可抵消过拟合：最强配置（ε=5）相比零样本降低MAPE 31%，相比最佳传统基线降低26%，同时通过每轮(ε,δ)-差分隐私约束信息泄漏。此外模型紧凑、更新量小，适合边缘AI部署。

Conclusion: 在联邦TSFM微调中，隐私与准确性可以互补而非相互竞争；DP噪声在保护隐私的同时起到正则化作用，改善模型泛化性能。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) such as Chronos have demonstrated strong forecasting capabilities across domains, yet adapting them to institutionally fragmented settings, where data cannot be centralized due to regulatory, competitive, or sovereignty constraints, remains unexplored. We introduce FedChronos, a framework for federated parameter-efficient fine-tuning of an already pre-trained TSFM, a setting that existing federated time-series work has not addressed, since prior methods either pre-train from scratch or align prototypes rather than adapt a fixed backbone. Our approach applies Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to the Chronos-T5 backbone and trains across distributed clients using FedAvg and FedProx, transmitting only lightweight adapter weights (384~KB per round, an 86$\times$ reduction over full-model exchange). We evaluate FedChronos on daily commodity prices from 15 Indian agricultural markets across 9 states, a naturally non-IID federated setting, and find that naïve LoRA fine-tuning overfits substantially on small per-client datasets, dropping below zero-shot performance. We further observe that differential privacy (DP) noise can act as implicit regularization and counteract this overfitting: in our experiments the strongest configuration ($\varepsilon = 5$) reduces mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) by 31% over zero-shot and 26% over the best traditional baseline, while bounding each round's information leakage via per-round $(\varepsilon, δ)$-differential privacy. Because the model is compact and the updates are small, the approach also suits edge AI deployments where both the network link and the client device are constrained. Overall, our findings suggest that privacy and accuracy can be complementary rather than competing objectives in federated TSFM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [172] [AlphaG-OPD: Reliability-Gated Sibling Counterfactuals for On-Policy Distillation in Symbolic Alpha Factor Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01303)
*Yaoyu Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaG-OPD，一种结构化的on-policy蒸馏框架，将符号Alpha因子发现的终端奖励转化为局部动作指导，通过三个子组件分别解决"在何处教"、"教什么可靠"、"教多强多久"的问题，在CSI300/500/1000及S&P500等多个市场取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号Alpha因子发现虽然能对完整表达式打分，但无法为生成该表达式的中间结构决策提供直接标签。GFlowNets虽然能在完整表达式上保持奖励比例分布，但其轨迹级目标函数不会在中间状态比较未被选中的兄弟动作，导致学习信号稀疏且缺乏局部指导。

Method: 提出了AlphaG-OPD框架，分为三个组件：(I) 在正向策略访问的部分AST状态暴露语法合法的兄弟节点，确定教学位置；(II) 在四个共享后缀下评估三个受支持的兄弟节点，当匹配比较表现出足够的胜者一致性和正的经验置信下界时，才接受KL有界的目标，确定可靠教学内容；(III) 通过有界重放、得分索引过期和前向梯度平衡来巩固已接受的目标，无需额外的因子评估。终端奖励、轨迹平衡、反向策略、语法和因子池规则均保持不变。

Result: 通过在CSI300、CSI500、CSI1000和S&P500上的广泛实验，并配合等物理评分的四臂消融测试（配对教学、可靠性门控和巩固策略），完整方法在多个随机种子下展现了强大的跨市场表现。

Conclusion: AlphaG-OPD通过将终端级因子评估转化为局部结构指导，有效提升了GFlowNets在符号Alpha因子发现中的学习效率，且无需修改终端奖励或其他核心组件，在跨市场场景中表现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: Symbolic alpha factor discovery can score a completed expression, but it provides no direct label for the structural decisions that produced it. Generative flow networks (GFlowNets) preserve a diverse, reward-proportional distribution over complete expressions, yet their trajectory-level objective does not compare unchosen sibling actions at an intermediate state. We introduce AlphaG-OPD, a structural on-policy distillation framework that turns terminal factor evaluations into local action guidance. Its design separates three decisions. Component I determines where to teach by exposing grammar-valid siblings at partial abstract-syntax-tree (AST) states visited by the current forward policy. Component II determines what is reliable enough to teach: it evaluates three supported siblings under four shared suffixes and admits a KL-bounded target only when their matched comparisons exhibit sufficient winner agreement and a positive empirical lower confidence bound (LCB). Component III determines how strongly and for how long to teach by consolidating accepted targets through bounded replay, score-indexed expiry, and forward-gradient balancing, without additional factor evaluations. Terminal reward, Trajectory Balance, the backward policy, grammar, and factor-pool rules remain unchanged. An equal-physical-score four-arm ablation tests paired teaching, reliability gating, and consolidation. Across China's CSI300, CSI500, and CSI1000 and the U.S. S&P 500, the complete method delivers strong cross-market performance over multiple random seeds.

</details>


### [173] [Spatiotemporal Proximal Causal Inference under Hidden Confounding and Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01352)
*Omar Faruque,Pavan Raj Ravi,Jianwu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的时空近端因果推断框架，通过引入处理诱导和结果诱导代理变量以及时空结果混杂桥函数，在存在隐藏混杂因素和干扰的时空数据中识别因果效应，并设计了基于Transformer编码器和条件互信息判别器的神经架构来实现该识别结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的时空数据因果效应估计面临两大挑战：隐藏混杂因素和干扰（interference）。标准的因果识别方法依赖于观测协变量条件下的条件可交换性假设，但当隐藏混杂因素同时影响处理和结果时该假设失效。这一问题在气候、环境政策、流行病学和区域经济学等领域普遍存在。

Method: 提出了时空近端因果推断框架，核心包括：(1) 引入处理诱导和结果诱导代理变量，联合捕获局部和邻域级混杂信息；(2) 推导时空结果混杂桥函数，无需直接恢复隐藏混杂因素即可识别潜在结果；(3) 在代理排除限制和时空完备性条件下建立桥函数的可辨识性；(4) 通过近端g-计算公式的推广恢复结果；(5) 设计基于Transformer时空编码器的神经架构，结合条件互信息判别器来强制执行排除限制，以及矩匹配网络保证桥函数满足识别方程；(6) 引入稳定加权方案处理处理支持不平衡问题。

Result: 在合成数据集上的实验表明，该方法与基线因果推断方法性能相当，同时是首个在存在时空干扰的隐藏混杂场景下通过近端因果推断框架提供理论保证结果的方法。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将近端因果推断理论扩展到时空设置，为时空干扰与隐藏混杂并存的因果效应估计提供了理论可辨识性保障和可操作的神经实现方案，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from real-world spatiotemporal data is challenging due to hidden confounders and interference. Standard causal identification methods assume conditional exchangeability given observed covariates, which fails whenever hidden confounders affect both treatment and outcomes - a common setting in domains such as climate, environmental policy, epidemiology, and regional economics. In this paper, we propose a novel spatiotemporal proximal causal inference framework that extends proximal identification theory to spatiotemporal settings. The proposed method jointly captures local and neighborhood-level confounding information by introducing treatment- and outcome-inducing proxies, and we derive a spatiotemporal outcome confounding bridge function that identifies the potential outcome without requiring direct recovery of the hidden confounder. We establish the identifiability of this bridge function under proxy exclusion restrictions and a spatiotemporal completeness condition, and show that the resulting estimator recovers the outcome through a proximal generalization of the g-computation formula. To operationalize this identification result, we propose a neural architecture that learns proxies via transformer-based spatiotemporal encoders - coupled with a conditional mutual information critic to enforce exclusion restrictions and a moment-matching network to guarantee that the learned bridge function satisfies the underlying identifying equation. We further introduce a stabilized weighting scheme to address treatment support imbalance. Experiments on synthetic datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves comparable performance to baseline causal inference methods, while providing, to our knowledge, the first theoretically grounded outcomes for the hidden confounding in the presence of spatiotemporal interference through a proximal causal inference framework.

</details>


### [174] [Do Neural Networks Really Beat the Curse of Dimensionality? A Bit-Complexity View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01357)
*Tong Mao,Jinchao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二进制编码和度量熵的统一近似框架，从计算比特复杂度的角度重新审视各类近似方法（经典方法和神经网络方法）的逼近效率，指出多数经典方法在比特度量下相对度量熵决定的内在极限是次优的，而神经网络看似优势实则源于函数类复杂度的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逼近理论以参数数量或自由度为衡量标准，忽略了实际计算中参数必须用有限比特编码的事实。因此需要从计算比特复杂度的角度重新评估近似效率，这将逼近效率与函数类的度量熵内在联系起来。

Method: 建立基于二进制编码和度量熵的统一近似框架，系统分析经典方法（多项式逼近、稀疏网格、有限元）以及浅层和深层神经网络，在具有可比度量熵的函数类上比较它们的逼近率。

Result: 在比特度量下，多数经典方法相对度量熵限制是次优的；神经网络可能表现出不同行为，但当复杂度以比特而非参数衡量时，没有任何方法能从根本上超越经典方法的逼近阶。许多神经网络的表面优势（如维度无关速率和超收敛现象）源于函数类复杂度差异而非架构优越性。

Conclusion: 传统的维数灾难可能具有误导性，根本限制是受度量熵支配的比特复杂度之诅咒，而非维数本身。这意味着评估逼近方法时应以比特复杂度为基准，而非单纯参数数量。

Abstract: Traditional approximation theory measures convergence rates in terms of the number of parameters or degrees of freedom. However, practical computation operates under finite precision: parameters must be encoded using a finite number of bits. Therefore, approximation efficiency should be evaluated in terms of computational bit complexity, which is intrinsically connected to the metric entropy of the underlying function class.
  In this work, we develop a unified approximation framework based on binary encoding and metric entropy. We analyze classical methods (including polynomial approximation, sparse grids, and finite elements) as well as shallow and deep neural networks, and compare their approximation rates for function classes with comparable metric entropy. We observe that, when evaluated in terms of bits, most classical methods are in general suboptimal relative to the intrinsic limits dictated by metric entropy, while neural network methods may exhibit different behaviors. We show that when complexity is measured in bits rather than parameters, no method fundamentally exceeds the approximation order achieved by classical approaches.
  Our results also indicate that many seeming advantages of neural networks, including dimension-independent rates and superconvergence phenomena, stem from differences in function class complexity rather than intrinsic architectural superiority. In this sense, the traditional curse of dimensionality can be misleading; the fundamental limitation is instead a curse of bit complexity, governed by metric entropy.

</details>


### [175] [When May a Model Replace the Experiment? Audits, Licenses, and the Price of Trust in Surrogate-Driven Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01378)
*Shuangxiu,Ma,Wenhe,Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器学习代理模型在科学设计任务中替代真实评估（实验/模拟/训练）的安全性问题。通过数学分析和三个穷尽真值标注的设计任务验证，作者证明了预测准确率（R²）不能作为可信度的锚点，提出了"结构规则"（预测可用于提案和训练，但认证结论必须基于真实评估）作为充分且必要的安全条件，并推导出代理模型可作为oracle的最小准则（秩保持而非准确性），同时提出了选择感知审计方法以量化信任成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在化学、材料科学和机器学习的设计活动中，真实评估代价高昂（实验、第一性原理模拟或完整训练），导致代理模型被广泛用于不仅提出候选方案，还用于评级甚至将自身预测反馈回搜索流程作为"测量值"使用。作者旨在严格界定这种替代实践的适用范围和风险边界，回答何时安全、安全证书的成本是多少、以及何时替代确实划算这三个核心问题。

Method: 数学分析结合三个穷尽真值标注的设计任务进行验证。作者建立了"选择税"（selection tax）的上下界，证明筛选N个候选会使被选中的候选的过度预测系统性膨胀；推导了安全性的充分必要条件（结构规则）；证明唯一可允许的oracle准则是秩保持（rank preservation）而非预测准确性；设计了最优查询复杂度的选择感知审计方案；论证了审计代理何时能降低认证评估成本的二分法。在六个任务-制度条件下执行432次代理拟合验证。

Result: 近完美的R²可以与最坏情况的选择结果兼容；筛选N个候选会产生有匹配上下界的可量化"选择税"。审计统计量在部署搜索性能上的Spearman秩相关为0.80-0.99，而R²与部署遗憾的秩相关最低仅0.33；审计筛选使认证oracle成本降低实测25倍。结构规则被证明是充分且必要的安全条件。

Conclusion: 预测准确性不能作为信任的锚点；安全的替代依赖于架构规则（认证必须基于真实评估），信任必须通过选择感知审计来购买；在满足秩保持准则且经过审计的条件下，代理模型可以显著降低认证评估成本（实测25倍），同时保持安全保证。

Abstract: Design campaigns in chemistry, materials science, and machine learning share a bottleneck: determining how good a candidate truly is requires an expensive evaluation - an experiment, a first-principles simulation, or a full training run. Machine-learning surrogates that predict these outcomes are increasingly used not only to propose candidates but to grade them, and even to feed their own predictions back into the search as though they were measurements. Through mathematical analysis validated on three exhaustively ground-truthed design tasks, we establish when this practice is safe, what any certificate of safety must cost, and when the substitution provably pays. Predictive accuracy cannot anchor trust: near-perfect R^2 is compatible with worst-possible selections, and screening N candidates inflates the over-prediction at the selected candidate by a quantifiable "selection tax" with matching upper and lower bounds. Safety follows instead from an architectural rule - predictions may propose and train without restriction, but every certified conclusion must rest on true evaluations - which is sufficient with no assumptions on the surrogate, and necessary, since admitting predictions into certification with the standing of measurements opens a deterministic self-confirmation failure mode. We derive the minimal criterion under which a model may act as an oracle (rank preservation, not accuracy), show that trust must be purchased through selection-aware audits that are optimal in query complexity, and prove a dichotomy fixing when audited surrogates cut certified evaluation cost. Across 432 surrogate fits over six task-regime conditions, the audit statistic tracks deployed search performance at Spearman rank correlation 0.80-0.99, while the rank correlation of R^2 with deployed regret falls as low as 0.33; audited screening reduces certified oracle cost by a measured factor of 25.

</details>


### [176] [On the Identifiability of Masked Prediction: Mode Blindness and Mask Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01383)
*Yichao Cai,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究掩码预测（masked prediction）中，当数据具有两个分离良好的全局模态时，接近最优的条件预测是否能够确定底层联合分布的问题。研究发现答案完全由掩码调度（mask schedule）决定：大型上下文掩码对全局模态权重"盲目"，而低可见性掩码可以恢复可辨识性，正的全掩码质量则无需任何数据假设即可锚定联合分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码预测通过拟合调度加权条件分布族来学习表示，但尚不清楚接近最优的条件预测何时能确定底层联合分布。对于具有两个分离全局模态的数据（超出快速混合恢复保证的范围），这个问题一直没有明确答案，需要系统性研究掩码调度如何影响联合分布的可辨识性。

Method: (1) 提出ε-可辨识性模量（ε-identifiability modulus），即与给定超额风险一致的最大分布误差；(2) 证明大型上下文主导的掩码调度对模态权重盲目，即使超额风险指数级小，分布误差仍是宏观的；(3) 通过精确的信息分解表明模态权重敏感性由可见上下文下的残余模态不确定性决定；(4) 在三个层面进行实证验证：可计算分布的枚举、梯度训练、以及真实语料库测量。

Result: 大型上下文掩码在指数级小的超额风险下仍存在宏观分布误差，证明其无法辨识全局模态权重；低可见性掩码可恢复模态权重敏感性；正的全掩码质量可在所有允许模型上锚定联合分布。实证结果验证了理论预测的速率，梯度训练复现了模态盲区和恢复现象，真实语料处于两个认证机制之间。

Conclusion: 掩码预测的可辨识性完全由掩码调度决定，而非数据本身的分布特性。大型上下文掩码盲区不可通过增加数据缓解，需要依赖低可见性掩码或全掩码来恢复可辨识性；真实文本数据恰好处于这两个已认证机制之间，为实际掩码训练的调度设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Masked prediction learns representations by fitting a schedule-weighted family of conditional laws, but it remains unclear when near-optimal conditional prediction pins down the underlying joint law. We study this question for data with two well-separated global modes, outside the reach of rapid-mixing recovery guarantees, and show that the answer is decided by the mask schedule alone. Under large-context mode pinning, reweighting the two modes can move the joint law by a constant in total variation while perturbing the masked objective exponentially little in the visible-context size: mask schedules dominated by large contexts are provably blind to the global mode weights. To quantify this, we introduce an $\varepsilon$-identifiability modulus, the largest distributional error consistent with a given excess risk, and prove that it remains macroscopic at an excess risk that is exponentially small. An exact information decomposition pinpoints what restores identifiability: mode-weight sensitivity is governed by the residual mode uncertainty given the visible context. Consequently, low-visibility masks recover this sensitivity, and positive full-mask mass anchors the joint law over all admissible models with no assumption on the data law. Empirically, we test our theory at three levels: enumeration on computable laws verifies the predicted rates, gradient training reproduces both the mode blindness and the recovery, and measurements on real corpora place natural text between the two certified regimes.

</details>


### [177] [TabDPT-Turbo: Efficient In-Context Learning for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01400)
*Rasa Hosseinzadeh,Alex Labach,Zexin Xue,Shuyi Han,Valentin Thomas,Anthony L. Caterini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TabDPT-Turbo（即TabDPT v1.2），一种基于行注意力机制和长上下文预训练的表格基础模型，通过消除检索需求，在保持与TabDPT v1.1相当性能的同时，实现了数量级的推理速度提升，并在主流基础模型中速度最快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型为追求原始性能，采用基于单元格的架构或检索机制，牺牲了推理效率，在计算资源受限或对推理速度敏感的场景中实用性受限。作者希望在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅提升推理速度。

Method: 采用行注意力机制（row-based attention），摒弃检索方案，结合长上下文预训练来弥补检索功能；同时加入架构改进和自监督预训练（SSL），并在新收集的更大规模真实数据语料上进行预训练。

Result: TabDPT-Turbo在TabArena-Lite、CC18和CTR23基准上取得了与TabDPT v1.1相当的默认性能，但推理速度快了数量级，在主流基础模型中速度最快。模型已以TabDPT v1.2名义开源发布。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过行注意力与长上下文预训练的结合，可以在不依赖检索的情况下实现兼具高性能和高速度的表格基础模型，为计算受限环境中的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models, driven by in-context learning, have rapidly grown in quality and popularity. However, recent approaches with either cell-based architectures or retrieval have sacrificed efficiency for raw performance, restricting their utility in situations where compute is limited or inference speed is crucial. We adopt an alternate approach, sticking with row-based attention while incorporating long context pre-training to eliminate the need for retrieval. By combining this with architectural improvements and SSL pre-training on a newly-sourced, larger corpus of real data results, we present TabDPT-Turbo, a model that provides comparable default performance to TabDPT v1.1 on TabArena-Lite, CC18, and CTR23, at orders of magnitude faster. In our experiments, TabDPT-Turbo is the fastest model overall among leading foundation models. We have released the new model as TabDPT v1.2 at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/TabDPT-inference.

</details>


### [178] [Cluster-Aware Over-the-Air Federated Learning with Energy-Harvesting Devices: From Global Training to Model Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01426)
*Furkan Bagci,Busra Tegin,Mohammad Kazemi,Tolga M. Duman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，在能量采集（EH）移动设备参与的空口联邦学习（OTA FL）中，利用用户聚类信息同时支持全局模型训练（通过能量与多样性感知调度减少数据偏差）和个性化模型训练（通过聚类结构定义簇级学习目标与OTA恢复目标），在异构数据分布下提升公平性或个性化性能并降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在无线网络中面临数据异构、通信资源有限和能量供给不稳定等关键挑战。现有工作未能在能量采集设备和异构数据分布的实际情况中，统一处理全局模型训练的公平性提升与个性化模型学习这两个密切相关但目标不同的需求。

Method: 本文在统一框架下研究两种学习模式：(1) 全局训练模式：利用聚类信息指导能量感知和多样性感知的调度策略，使被调度的活跃用户提供更具代表性的聚合更新；(2) 个性化模式：利用聚类结构定义簇级学习目标和空口OTA恢复目标，通过无线多址信道上的同时传输，使参数服务器训练多个簇特定的模型。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的统一框架能够根据运行模式改善公平性或个性化性能，同时降低通信开销。

Conclusion: 通过将聚类信息融入OTA FL的调度与聚合设计中，提出的框架在能量采集和异构数据条件下同时兼顾了全局模型的代表性与局部模型的个性化需求，实现了通信效率与学习性能的权衡优化。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables distributed optimization and learning across decentralized edge devices while preserving data privacy, but its performance is fundamentally constrained by heterogeneous data distributions, limited communication resources, and energy availability. In practical wireless networks, mobile devices (MDs) often exhibit diverse data and learning objectives, naturally forming clusters of users with jointly trainable models. When devices rely on energy harvesting (EH), stochastic energy arrivals further complicate participation and scheduling under communication constraints. In this work, we study over-the-air (OTA) FL with EH MDs under heterogeneous data distributions, and investigate two closely related learning objectives within a unified framework: one aiming for a more representative global model by reducing data bias, and the other learning more personalized cluster-specific models by exploiting this bias. In the global training mode, cluster information guides energy- and diversity-aware scheduling, ensuring that the scheduled active users provide a more representative aggregate update. In the personalization mode, the same cluster structure defines cluster-level learning objectives and OTA recovery targets, enabling the parameter server to train multiple cluster-specific models through simultaneous transmissions over the wireless multiple-access channel. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed unified framework improves fairness or personalization, depending on the operating mode, while reducing communication overhead.

</details>


### [179] [Statistical Mechanics of Learning on Product Wasserstein Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01434)
*Srinivasa Rao P Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将权重分布约束视为内在几何而非容量限制，将深度神经网络和变分量子电路分别建模为经典/量子Wasserstein流形上的梯度流，并引入两种实用算法（Hierarchical DisCo-SGD和Quantum DisCo），实验表明该方法能改善泛化、稳定训练并减轻量子贫瘠高原问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计力学将权重分布约束视为缩小解空间、降低模型容量的限制。本文采取相反视角，基于分布约束感知机的前期工作，提出将预设的权重分布视为学习自然展开的内在几何，而非仅仅是限制条件。

Method: 将深度神经网络和变分量子电路分别形式化为经典Wasserstein流形（每层一个）和量子Wasserstein流形上的梯度流。开发了深度网络的层次平均场描述，并利用1阶量子Wasserstein距离将框架扩展到量子设置。提出了两种实用算法：Hierarchical DisCo-SGD和Quantum DisCo，它们近似遵循流形乘积上的测地线。

Result: 在教师-学生问题、标准图像分类任务和小型变分量子分类器上的实验表明，尊重这些分布几何能够改善泛化能力、稳定训练过程，并相比无约束和纯范数基线的基准，减轻贫瘠高原的严重程度。

Conclusion: 该方法首次将结构约束重新定义为几何先验，为将生物、频谱或硬件来源的分布信息融入经典和量子学习系统提供了新路径。

Abstract: Normally the statistical mechanics of learning treats constraints on weight distributions as restrictions that shrink the space of possible solutions. Therefore, it reduces model capacity. In this paper we would like to take a contrary approach, which, however, is based on the earlier work on distribution-constrained perceptrons. Rather than treating a prescribed weight distribution as a mere restriction, we propose that it defines the intrinsic geometry upon which learning naturally unfolds. We formulate both deep neural networks and variational quantum circuits as gradient flows on a product of Wasserstein manifolds -- one classical Wasserstein space for each layer and one quantum Wasserstein space for the circuit parameters. Within this geometry, the capacity reduction, which was previously associated with distributional constraints, appears as the metric structure of the constraint manifold itself. We develop a hierarchical mean-field description for deep networks, extend the framework to the quantum setting using the quantum Wasserstein distance of order 1, and introduce two such practical algorithms, Hierarchical DisCo-SGD and Quantum DisCo, that follow approximate geodesics on the manifold of the product itself. Experiments on teacher-student problems, standard image classification tasks, and small variational quantum classifiers show that respecting these distributional geometries improves generalization, stabilizes training, and reduces the severity of barren plateaus compared with unconstrained and purely norm-based baselines. This approach firstly reframes structural constraints as geometric priors and suggests a route for incorporating biological, spectral, or hardware-derived distributional information into both learning systems, viz., classical and quantum learning.

</details>


### [180] [Conformalized Large Language Models under Configuration Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01460)
*Yuqicheng Zhu,Jialin Yu,Lin Li,Gengyuan Zhang,Zhen Yang,Steffen Staab,Puneet Dokania,Philip Torr,Jie Tang,Evgeny Kharlamov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了配置偏移对LLM共形预测有效性的影响，发现提示模板、解码温度和权重量化等配置变化会持续侵蚀覆盖保证，并提出了基于边界启发和脆弱性感知的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测（CP）为LLM提供有限样本覆盖保证，但非一致性分数往往由推理管线诱导，不仅依赖数据分布，还依赖提示模板、解码参数和部署设置等可配置因素。这些配置在实践中频繁修改但很少被视为偏移来源，其影响尚不明确。

Method: 将配置偏移沿三条轴线系统研究：提示模板、解码温度和权重量化。在9个LLM、4个数据集和4个非一致性分数上开展广泛实证研究，推导覆盖下界以归因损失，并使用有限样本插件版本作为偏移严重性的经验诊断工具。提出两种缓解方法：边界启发的重校准（需有限测试样本）和脆弱性感知的校准集成（无需测试数据）。

Result: 配置偏移持续侵蚀CP有效性，经常使经验覆盖率低于目标；但效率基本得以保留，有效预测集大小与i.i.d.基线接近。边界启发的重校准使用有限测试样本即有效，脆弱性感知的校准集成无需测试数据即可恢复大部分丢失的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 配置偏移是LLM共形预测中一个被忽视但严重的问题，需要被系统对待；所提出的诊断工具和缓解策略可以实际帮助恢复覆盖保证。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) is a distribution-free framework for uncertainty quantification that has recently been adapted to large language models (LLMs), providing prediction sets with finite-sample coverage guarantees under exchangeability. Yet for LLMs, nonconformity scores are often induced by an inference pipeline, not just a fixed model, making them depend not only on the data distribution but also on configurable factors such as the prompt template, decoding parameters, and deployment setting. Since such configurations are routinely modified in practice but rarely treated as a source of shift, their impact on CP validity remains poorly understood. We call this \emph{configuration shift} and study it systematically along three axes: prompt template, decoding temperature, and weight quantization. In a broad empirical study spanning $9$ LLMs, $4$ datasets, and $4$ nonconformity scores, we find that configuration shift consistently erodes CP validity, often driving empirical coverage below the target. By contrast, efficiency is largely preserved: valid prediction sets remain close in size to the i.i.d. baseline. We derive coverage lower bounds that attribute this loss to a discrepancy between calibration and test score distributions, and use their finite-sample plug-in versions as empirical diagnostics of shift severity. We further show that these findings lead to practical mitigations: bound-inspired recalibration is effective with limited test examples, while fragility-aware calibration ensembling recovers much of the lost coverage without test data.

</details>


### [181] [Plasticity of Growing and Elastic Neural Networks in Online Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01475)
*Jeong Min Kong,Richard S. Sutton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了生长型神经网络和弹性神经网络在在线持续学习中的可塑性（plasticity）保持问题，发现自适应生长网络和自适应弹性网络均能在不损失可塑性的情况下保持高预测准确率，其中弹性网络还能维持近恒定的紧凑网络规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然生长/弹性神经网络在离线持续学习中已被用于缓解灾难性遗忘，但可塑性丧失同样是持续学习的重大挑战。鉴于在线持续学习更接近动物学习方式，且随机初始化隐藏单元的增量引入有助于保持可塑性，作者希望系统性地研究这些网络在在线持续学习中的可塑性表现。

Method: 在监督学习环境中，对多种基础生长型（growing）和弹性型（elastic）神经网络进行在线持续学习实验。自适应生长网络逐步增量添加随机初始化的新隐藏单元，同时保持所有现有连接可自适应更新；自适应弹性网络在此基础上，在每个新任务开始时额外剪除已估计为死掉的隐藏单元。

Result: 自适应生长网络即使死隐藏单元比例持续增加，仍能保持高预测准确率且不丧失可塑性；自适应弹性网络在剪除死单元的同时，能实现优异的准确率、保持可塑性，并维持近恒定且紧凑的网络规模。

Conclusion: 生长型和弹性神经网络因其能够根据学习目标自适应调整网络结构，有望成为在线持续学习中保持高可塑性的有前景的算法类别。

Abstract: Neural networks that can grow or both grow and shrink during learning, referred to as growing neural networks and elastic neural networks, respectively, have recently been explored in offline continual learning with a particular focus on catastrophic forgetting. Driven by the observations that 1) online continual learning closely resembles how animals learn; 2) loss of plasticity---the progressive decline in a learning network's ability to learn---is another crucial challenge facing continual learning; and 3) incremental introduction of randomly initialized hidden units was recently shown to help preserve plasticity, in this paper, we study the plasticity of several foundational growing and elastic networks in online continual learning. Our experiments in supervised learning settings show that adaptive growing networks, which incrementally incorporate new, randomly initialized units to the network while keeping all existing connections adaptive, can maintain high prediction accuracy without losing plasticity despite the continuous increase in the dead hidden unit proportion. Furthermore, we demonstrate that adaptive elastic networks, which in addition to progressively adding new hidden units also prune estimated dead hidden units at the beginning of each new task, can achieve excellent accuracy without loss of plasticity while simultaneously maintaining a near-constant, compact size. Our results suggest that growing and elastic networks, which exhibit the ability to adapt its structure to the relevant learning objectives, can be a promising class of algorithms also for preserving high plasticity in online continual learning.

</details>


### [182] [Interpretable MEG Decoding of Perceived Speech: Cortical Sources and the Stimulus Features That Drive Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01481)
*Ilia Semenkov,Daria Kleeva,Ivan Dakhtin,Zarina Maksudova,Alex Ossadtchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文改进了基于MEG的语音检索模型，将空间注意力替换为球谐函数、减少分支数量并增加时间滤波，使模型权重可映射到源空间，从而揭示语音检索的神经机制和关键语音特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MEG-to-audio检索模型虽然性能高，但其权重无法映射到电生理学量，且不清楚哪些语音属性驱动了检索行为，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 重新设计模型前端和解码器：用球谐函数替代扁平传感器布局上的空间注意力；将270个分支减少到25个并添加时间滤波以匹配神经源；使卷积解码器更浅；训练前移除眼动和心电成分。结合MEG遮挡和随机词表对照实验分析特征贡献。

Result: 在MEG-MASC上达到39.75±0.34%的Top-1准确率（1005个候选中），解码器参数减少约20倍。权重映射到源空间后恢复出语音感知网络生成器，左侧半球的多个分支携带更高的高频节律成分。15/19个刺激特征有贡献，静音、声音强度、元音和声学起始影响最大。wav2vec目标可压缩至约12个特征维度而不损失精度。

Conclusion: 通过源映射和输入干预相结合，研究者成功揭示了MEG语音检索的驱动因素：神经源定位和具体语音特征的作用得到阐明，说明模型的可解释性和性能可以兼得。

Abstract: Short segments of perceived speech can be retrieved from non-invasive magnetoencephalographic (MEG) recordings by deep networks trained with a CLIP-style objective against wav2vec 2.0 audio embeddings. Yet their weights do not map onto electrophysiological quantities, and it remains unclear which speech properties drive retrieval.
  We build on a high-performing MEG-to-audio retrieval architecture but redesign both its front end and decoder. Its spatial attention operates on a flattened sensor layout; we replace it with spherical harmonics defined on the three-dimensional MEG helmet geometry. We reduce the subject-specific representation from 270 to 25 branches, add a temporal filter to each branch to match it to a neuronal source in space and time, and make the convolutional decoder shallower. Ocular and cardiac components are removed before training to reduce the risk of stimulus-locked shortcuts.
  On MEG-MASC, the model reaches 39.75 +/- 0.34% Top-1 accuracy among 1005 candidates across six trained solutions, with about 20 times fewer decoder parameters. Its weights map to source space, recovering generators consistent with the speech-perception network, while left-lateralized branches carry higher-frequency rhythmic components not evident on the right. Paired MEG occlusion shows that 15 of 19 stimulus features contribute, with the largest effects for silence, sound intensity, vowels, and acoustic onsets. Random word lists behave oppositely: substituting narrative MEG into them improves retrieval, indicating that activity without narrative structure carries less recoverable information than activity during coherent speech. The wav2vec target can be reduced to about twelve learned feature dimensions without loss of accuracy, whereas strong temporal compression causes a clear loss.
  Together, source mapping and input interventions reveal what drives retrieval.

</details>


### [183] [BiKAN: Restoring Collapsed Basis of Binary Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01490)
*Kazi Ahmed Asif Fuad,Lizhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BiKAN，解决二值化KAN网络中因激活值限制为{-1,+1}导致的多项式基函数坍缩（称为"空间正交性坍缩"）问题，通过引入2阶Walsh字符（parity平面）恢复显式的成对坐标交互，在CIFAR-10等任务上显著提升精度，且硬件效率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值化KAN不仅改变参数精度，还改变了每层可用的函数空间。当激活值被限制在{-1,+1}时，基函数坍缩为常数和一阶响应，导致网络丢失高阶特征表达能力。作者将这一结构性缺陷命名为"空间正交性坍缩"（Spatial Orthogonality Collapse），并致力于修复该问题。

Method: BiKAN在每个二值KAN层中增强所选2阶Walsh字符（parity平面）。通过固定的环形通道卷产生成对奇偶校验，然后利用与现有W1A1路径相同的XNOR-popcount操作，通过学习到的二值投影混合这些parity平面，从而无需学习路由或基于乘法器的特征生成即可恢复显式成对坐标。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，去除parity导致精度下降1.23个百分点（p=0.003）；在相同~11.9M参数预算下，parity比传统加宽提升3.09个百分点（p<10^-4）。W1A1下BiKAN在MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100分别达到99.48%、84.38%、55.81%。FPGA实现中，卷积设计将DSP从164降至72，延迟从401降至54.8ms；幂二感知稠密设计实现零DSP推理，仅损失0.03个百分点精度。

Conclusion: BiKAN通过引入2阶Walsh字符有效修复了二值KAN的空间正交性坍缩问题，在精度和硬件效率之间取得了优异平衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Binarizing a polynomial Kolmogorov--Arnold Network (KAN) not only changes parameter precision, but also alters the function space available to each layer. When activations are restricted to ${-1,+1}$, all even powers reduce to $1$ and all odd powers reduce to $x$, causing the elementwise polynomial basis to collapse to constant and first-order responses. We refer to this structural failure as Spatial Orthogonality Collapse. Our proposed BiKAN addresses this critical issue by augmenting each binary KAN layer with selected degree-2 Walsh characters. Fixed circular channel rolls generate pairwise parities, and learned binary projections mix them using the same XNOR--popcount operations as the remaining W1A1 paths. This restores explicit pairwise coordinates without learned routing or multiplier-based feature generation. Experiments on CIFAR-10 confirms that removing parity reduces accuracy by $1.23$ points over five paired seeds ($p=0.003$), the gain increases as width decreases, and accuracy improves monotonically as more parity planes are added. At an equal $\sim$11.9M-parameter budget, parity outperforms conventional widening by $3.09$ points ($p<10^{-4}$). At W1A1, BiKAN reaches $99.48\%$, $84.38\%$, and $55.81\%$ on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100, respectively. Post-route Zynq-7020 FPGA results show that the repair remains hardware-efficient; the convolutional design cuts DSP usage from 164 to 72 and estimated compute-core latency from 401 to 54.8 ms, while the power-of-two-aware dense design achieves zero-DSP inference with a 0.03-point accuracy loss. The BiKAN implementation is available at https://github.com/OSU-STARLAB/BiKAN.

</details>


### [184] [Stochastic Sequential Search in Very-High-Dimensional Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01502)
*Petr Somol,Jiří Grim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预算化采样步骤算子对，将传统顺序子集搜索（如浮点搜索）的全局扫描替换为固定数量的候选评估，形成随机序列搜索（SSS）家族。sSFFS在高维数据集上以约四分之一的评估成本保留97%以上的全搜索性能，并能在远超全搜索能力范围的数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序子集搜索（如带浮点回溯的前向选择及其后代）是特征选择的质量基准，但每一步都需要扫描全部剩余候选特征，这使其无法应用于超高维问题。在超高维场景下，仅有单特征排序可行，但其对特征间交互的建模能力很弱甚至完全没有。

Method: 引入预算化采样步骤算子对：用一个固定数量的候选评估取代全扫描。候选特征通过温度控制的softmax采样从依赖感知的逐特征统计量中抽取，统计来自每次准则评估并在线学习，同时由均匀探索下限保护。将此算子替换进任意顺序方法即可得到其随机对应物，定义了SSS家族。本文重点研究浮点搜索的随机对应物sSFFS。

Result: 在500维madelon数据集上，sSFFS在每个子集大小下保留至少97%的全SFFS准则值，仅需约四分之一的评估次数；在5,000维gisette上超越DAF和BIF排序方法；在10,105维reuters上，sSFFS在搜索目标和保留准确率上均支配BIF和DAF，且单核运算仅需约两分钟。

Conclusion: 预算化采样算子使顺序搜索家族能够扩展到超高维问题，同时保持对特征交互建模的能力。在500个训练样本下，超越顺序前沿的约束因素已从搜索转为准则本身。论文附带经过验证的独立实现代码。

Abstract: Sequential subset search -- forward selection with floating backtracking and its descendants -- remains the quality reference in feature selection, but every member of the family sweeps the full pool of remaining candidate features at each step, which excludes it from very-high-dimensional problems; there, only individual-feature ranking remains practical, and it models feature interplay weakly or not at all. We introduce a budgeted sampled step operator pair that replaces the full sweeps by a fixed number of candidate evaluations per step. Candidates are drawn by temperature-controlled softmax sampling from dependency-aware per-feature statistics learned online from every criterion evaluation the search performs, guarded by a uniform exploration floor; per-step cost becomes independent of dimensionality. Substituting the operators turns any sequential method into its stochastic counterpart, defining the Stochastic Sequential Search (SSS) family; we study the stochastic counterpart of floating search, sSFFS. On 500-dimensional madelon, sSFFS retains at least 97% of the full-SFFS criterion value at every subset size at about a quarter of its evaluations, while uniform sampling at the same budget collapses on madelon's synergistic features. On 5,000-dimensional gisette, far beyond full-SFFS reach, sSFFS exceeds the saturated criterion level of DAF and BIF ranking at matched budgets; holdout validation shows that at 500 training samples the binding constraint beyond the sequential frontier becomes the criterion, not the search. On 10,105-dimensional reuters, under a trustworthy multinomial filter criterion, sSFFS dominates BIF and DAF on the search objective and on holdout accuracy at every subset size, in about two minutes of single-core evaluation work. A verified standalone implementation accompanies the paper.

</details>


### [185] [Question Begets Question: Self-Evolving Curriculum for Reinforcement Fine-Tuning on Competition Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01522)
*Longtian Bao,Jianyou Wang,Yang Zhang,Youze Zheng,Ramamohan Paturi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型技能学习的三大障碍（数据稀缺、缺乏推理轨迹、性能天花板），提出Question-begets-Question（QbQ）数据生成方法和自演化课程学习策略，通过强化学习仅基于问题和答案训练Qwen2.5-Math-7B，在AIME数据集上从5.6%提升至16.5%的pass@1，突破了静态训练的性能天花板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型学习未掌握技能时面临三个核心困难：训练数据稀缺、无法获得ground-truth推理轨迹、模型存在看似无法突破的性能天花板（增加数据也无法提升）。作者希望系统性地理解和解决这些问题。

Method: 提出了两个核心方法：(1) QbQ（Question-begets-Question）：教师模型将现有问题转化为探索相同底层技能的多样化变体，解决数据稀缺问题；(2) 自演化课程学习：每轮评估当前模型，从模型能大部分正确解答的问题中生成QbQ变体用于训练，仅通过强化学习（问题和最终答案）训练，不使用教师推理轨迹。

Result: 静态训练（真实+合成数据增强）和非常规课程QbQ训练分别在pass@1上封顶于12.5%和14.5%；而自演化课程在相同数据预算下突破天花板，将pass@1提升至16.5%，经过20轮训练仍无饱和迹象。模型通过训练"能大部分做对"的问题变体，最终能解决训练中从未见过的更难问题。

Conclusion: 性能天花板并非模型固有能力限制，通过自演化课程学习策略可以突破。反直觉的发现是：训练模型解决其已能大部分正确回答的问题变体比训练更难的问题更有效，且这种训练能泛化到更难的问题上。这为语言模型技能获取提供了新的训练范式。

Abstract: Teaching a language model a skill it has not mastered is obstructed by three recurring difficulties: training data is scarce, ground-truth reasoning traces are usually unavailable, and models often exhibit an apparent ceiling beyond which additional data yields no further improvement. We study these difficulties in a controlled setting, fine-tuning Qwen2.5-Math-7B on competition mathematics (AIME), a task on which it initially solves only 5.6\% of problems (pass@1). To address data scarcity, we introduce Question-begets-Question (QbQ), a scalable procedure in which a teacher transforms existing problems into diverse variants that probe the same underlying skills; to model the absence of oracle reasoning, we train exclusively via reinforcement learning on problem statements and final answers, never on teacher reasoning traces. Static training on such data, however, plateaus well short of the task: real-plus-synthetic augmentation and non-curriculum QbQ generated synthetic data training cap pass@1 at 12.5\% and 14.5\% respectively, despite large increases in data. Our central finding is that this ceiling is not intrinsic to the model. We propose a self-evolving curriculum that, each round, evaluates the current checkpoint, seeds QbQ from the problems it can mostly get right, and trains on the resulting variants; under an identical data budget, this breaks the ceiling and lifts pass@1 to 16.5\% with no sign of saturation after 20 rounds. Counterintuitively, we find that models improve when trained on variants of problems they can mostly get right, and that models trained this way go on to solve harder problems never seen during training.

</details>


### [186] [Gram-Space: Structure-Preserving Codebook Compression for Memory-Efficient Neuro-Symbolic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01528)
*Weilun Wang,Wantong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gram-Space压缩框架，通过Gram-Schmidt正交化将高维码本向量表示为紧凑的正交坐标系，在保持矩阵型VSA算子点积结构的前提下，实现GPU内存最高15.75倍压缩和3.62倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量符号架构(VSA)广泛用于神经符号AI推理，但高维码本带来严重的内存瓶颈，限制了可扩展性和实际部署。

Method: 提出Gram-Space框架，应用Gram-Schmidt正交化将码本向量压缩到紧凑的正交坐标系中，保留矩阵型VSA算子所需的点积结构，支持矩阵相似度、概率向量化和注意力分数计算在数值上等效执行。

Result: 在标准神经符号推理数据集上的实验表明，Gram-Space在多种SOTA VSA模型上将模型级GPU内存使用最高降低15.75倍，推理延迟最高提升3.62倍。性能分析显示该框架减少了码本相关阶段的内存分配开销并提升了硬件利用率。

Conclusion: Gram-Space通过正交基表示在保持数学等价性的同时显著缓解了VSA的内存瓶颈，为神经符号工作负载提供了可扩展、高效的部署方案。

Abstract: Vector symbolic architectures (VSA) are widely used for reasoning in neuro-symbolic (NeSy) AI, yet high-dimensional codebooks often create severe memory bottlenecks that limit scalability and deployment. In this paper, we propose Gram-Space, a compression framework that applies Gram-Schmidt orthogonalization to represent codebook vectors in a compact orthonormal coordinate system. Gram-Space preserves the dot-product structure required by matrix-based VSA operators, which supports numerically equivalent execution of matrix similarity, probability vectorization, and attention score computations. We provide a correctness analysis showing that inner products are preserved under the orthonormal basis representation. Using modern GPU hardware, we benchmark the Gram-Space framework on standard neuro-symbolic reasoning datasets. Experimental evaluations across state-of-the-art VSA models show that Gram-Space reduces model-level GPU memory usage by up to 15.75x and improves inference latency by up to 3.62x. Profiling results further indicate that Gram-Space reduces allocation-heavy overhead in codebook-associated stages and improves hardware utilization for NeSy workloads.

</details>


### [187] [Rethinking Personalized Reward Modeling for LLMs under Preference Heterogeneity via Group-Debiased Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01556)
*Seongyoon Kim,Boryeong Cho,Jihwan Oh,Seokhyun Chung,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedGD方法，用于联邦学习中的奖励模型个性化。研究发现单个FedAvg模型在偏好组平衡时比分组训练更优，归因于共享初始化的平坦性；但组不平衡会破坏此效果。FedGD通过组去偏客户端采样来学习高适应性初始化，无需预先知道组的先验信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户偏好数据敏感且无法集中存储，联邦学习虽能解决隐私问题，但现有方法假设偏好异质性需要为每个客户端组单独训练初始化模型。作者质疑这一假设的必要性，探索是否可以用单个共享模型实现有效的个性化。

Method: 提出FedGD（Federated Learning with Group Debiasing），在联邦训练过程中自动发现潜在偏好组，通过组去偏客户端采样来学习单一奖励模型。核心思想是通过平衡各组客户端的采样权重，抵消组不平衡带来的不对称影响，保持初始化的平坦性。

Result: 在平衡偏好组条件下，单个FedAvg模型仅需少量本地优化步骤即可超越每个真实组单独训练的奖励模型。但组不平衡会导致少数族客户端离决策边界太远无法恢复。FedGD通过组去偏采样有效解决了这一问题。

Conclusion: 偏好异质性并不需要为每个组单独初始化模型。共享初始化通过跨组平均学习丰富的共享表征并抵消冲突的偏好方向，其平坦性使其可以快速适应个性化需求。FedGD通过组去偏采样消除了组不平衡的负面影响，实现了无需组先验知识的高适应性个性化。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly aligned to human preferences via reward modeling, but user preference data are sensitive and often cannot be centralized. Federated learning keeps such data local while learning a shared initial reward model, which is later personalized for each client through local fine-tuning. Because users often assign opposite labels to the same pair of responses, existing federated methods address preference heterogeneity by clustering similar clients and training one reward model per group, assuming that each group requires its own initialization. We show that this assumption is unnecessary. Under balanced preference groups, a single FedAvg model, despite starting at nearly random accuracy, surpasses reward models trained separately for each ground-truth group after only a few local optimization steps. We attribute this phenomenon to the flatness of the shared initialization: averaging across all clients learns richer shared representations that distinguish responses while canceling conflicting preference directions, leaving the model near a decision boundary that can be rapidly adapted. Group imbalance breaks this effect as the cancellation becomes asymmetric and leaves minority clients too far from the boundary to recover. Motivated by this observation, we propose FedGD (Federated Learning with Group Debiasing), which discovers latent preference groups during federated training and learns a single reward model using group-debiased client sampling. By counteracting the effect of group imbalance, FedGD learns an initialization that remains highly adaptable, enabling effective personalization without prior knowledge of the underlying groups.

</details>


### [188] [Meganeura: Portable GPU Training and Inference through Vulkan and Metal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01563)
*Dzmitry Malyshau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Meganeura提出了一个紧凑的原生编译器，通过Vulkan和Metal在消费级GPU上同时支持训练和推理。在50个设备-工作负载-模式测试单元中48个通过验证，与PyTorch相比在严格f32下赢得12/20的最小延迟单元，编译时间仅0.1-2.4秒（对比torch.compile的6-96秒），二进制体积13MiB，并通过Android XR案例展示了从训练到部署的端到端可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练和部署推理经常跨越导出、转换和平台特定运行时边界，这种割裂增加了复杂性和部署成本。作者希望探索是否能用一个紧凑的原生编译器同时覆盖训练和推理两个阶段，在消费级GPU上实现统一的训练-部署栈。

Method: Meganeura是一个包含类型化静态图、自动微分、优化器、检查点、内存规划和运行时的编译器，通过Vulkan和Metal将专用程序下放到消费级GPU。实验与PyTorch在多种硬件（NVIDIA/AMD独显、AMD APU、Apple silicon、Intel iGPU）上对比五个匹配工作负载，协议区分严格f32和加速路径，并独立门控前向和后向。

Result: 50个测试单元中48个通过前后向门控；严格f32下20个GPU参考最小延迟单元中赢得12个，中位训练差距1.8x；AMD独显上4/5推理工作负载在1.10x内，3个训练负载更快；最差训练差距4.6x；编译时间0.1-2.4秒对比torch.compile的6-96秒；二进制仅13MiB。

Conclusion: 通用消费级图形API能够支持一个紧凑的共享训练到部署栈，性能有时可媲美厂商优化方案。测量到的性能差距源于内核覆盖、调度和算术策略问题，而非API本身的固有限制。

Abstract: Training and deployed inference often cross export, conversion, and platform-specific runtime boundaries. Meganeura asks whether one compact native compiler can span both phases on consumer GPUs. Its typed static graph, automatic differentiation, optimizer, checkpoint, memory planner, and runtime lower specialized programs through Vulkan and Metal.
  We compare five matched workloads with PyTorch on NVIDIA and AMD discrete GPUs, an AMD APU, Apple silicon, and an Intel iGPU. The protocol separates strict f32 from validated fast paths and gates forward and backward independently. Forty-eight of 50 device-workload-mode cells pass both gates; the other two share one unresolved backward-reference disagreement on a newly supported APU. In strict f32, Meganeura wins 12 of 20 GPU-referenced minimal-latency cells and has a median valid training gap of 1.8x. On the discrete AMD GPU, four of five inference workloads are within 1.10x of compiled ROCm PyTorch and three training workloads are faster. Under accelerated contracts, the worst training gap is 4.6x.
  Compilation takes 0.1-2.4 seconds versus 6-96 seconds for torch.compile on supported GPU paths; the stripped binary is 13 MiB. Dispatch profiles localize the largest gaps to convolution derivatives and attention backward. A physical Android XR case study transfers a Meganeura-trained decoder into an Adreno/OpenXR application sharing the graphics queue. The results show that general consumer graphics APIs can support a compact shared train-to-deploy stack at useful, sometimes vendor-competitive performance. The measured gaps point to kernel coverage, scheduling, and arithmetic policy rather than an identified API limitation.

</details>


### [189] [Measuring in-context algorithmic reasoning in language models against an exact Bayes-optimal standard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01575)
*Hector Zenil,Luan Ozelim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出F-ICL基准测试，基于图灵完备机器F（经sF对称化去除输出极性偏差）穷举15亿个程序并计算贝叶斯最优后验，用于评估大语言模型的归纳推理能力。测试发现45/46个模型的距离度分布比简单的按键参考更差，模型行为接近无环的打印机器混合而非带环的最优混合，且更新非单调（6545次由解转未解），该差距无法通过规模或准确率预测，推理后训练反而加剧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏正确归纳推理的真实标签，难以区分大语言模型是真正进行算法推理还是仅仅完成模式匹配，因此需要一个具有精确最优解作为ground truth的测试框架。

Method: 利用图灵完备机器F的补对称版本sF，穷举全部15亿个长度≤13的程序，在受限通用（Levin–Solomonoff）先验下闭式计算贝叶斯最优后验；每个任务配对其按位补任务使最优得分相同，原始-孪生差距隔离模型归纳偏差；对105个服务配置、37个开放模型（0.8B-675B）及四家实验室的前沿系统进行评分。

Result: 模型正确回答高达92%的查询，但45/46个模型的分布距最优比按键参考更远；模型行为被仅基于可见证据的低阶前缀统计所界定；更新非单调产生6545次解到未解的转变；差距与准确率无显著相关（ρ=-0.19），不随规模或前沿代际缩小，而推理后训练反而扩大差距。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在F-ICL上表现出系统性的归纳偏差，其行为更接近无环的统计混合而非贝叶斯最优的算法推理，且无论模型规模或训练后处理如何增强，这种偏差依然存在，表明大语言模型的上下文学习仍以模式完成为主导而非真正的算法推理。

Abstract: Whether large language models perform genuine algorithmic reasoning or mere pattern completion is hard to test, because most benchmarks lack a ground truth for correct inductive inference. We introduce F-ICL, an in-context-learning benchmark that supplies one exactly. Using the Turing-complete machine F, complement-symmetrised into sF to remove output-polarity bias, we exhaustively enumerate all 1.5 billion programs of length $L\le13$ and compute the Bayes-optimal posterior in closed form under a bounded universal (Levin--Solomonoff) prior; models are scored by how closely their served distributions approach it at matched evidence. Each task is paired with its bitwise complement, on which the optimum scores identically, so an original-twin gap isolates the model's inductive bias. Across 105 serving configurations spanning 37 open models (0.8B--675B) and frontier systems from four laboratories, models answer up to 92\% of queries correctly, yet 45 of 46 models yield distributions farther from the optimum than a keystroke reference, and their behaviour is bracketed by low-order prefix statistics fitted only on visible evidence. That reference is itself an algorithmic mixture, induced by a print-only machine with no loops, so the panel's implied measure sits closer to a loop-free mixture than to the loop-bearing optimum, independently of the reference machine. Updating is also non-monotone, which no prior explains: a Bayes-rational solved set can only grow in this realisable, noiseless setting, yet added examples produce $6{,}545$ solved-to-unsolved transitions against $13{,}702$ gains. The gap is not predicted by accuracy (Spearman $ρ=-0.19$, $p=0.21$), does not close with scale or across frontier generations in the serving modes that expose distributions, and is widened by instruction and reasoning post-training. F-ICL is released as an open, reproducible benchmark and toolkit.

</details>


### [190] [HindSearch: Trajectory-Level Hindsight Critique for Search-Augmented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01597)
*Haowei Liu,Jiamian Wang,Hsin-Tai Wu,Zhiqiang Tao,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HindSearch，一种用于GRPO的后见之明自蒸馏方法。通过让冻结的评判模型利用黄金答案为每个失败轨迹撰写简短批评，为学生的搜索动作提供辅助的在线蒸馏信号，在七个标准基准上达到39.4%的平均EM，超越现有搜索强化学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索增强的语言模型智能体通常使用二元的精确匹配奖励进行训练，这种训练方式浪费了失败轨迹中蕴含的大部分信息——失败轨迹并未告诉我们失败的原因。作者希望利用失败轨迹中的丰富信息来提升训练效果。

Method: 方法核心包括三点：(1) 在GRPO每次rollout后，使用一个冻结的评判模型（judge）根据黄金答案为每个失败轨迹撰写简短的批评；(2) 该批评为学生的搜索动作提供辅助的在线自蒸馏信号；(3) 通过消融实验验证后见之明信息（黄金答案）是关键贡献来源。

Result: 在包含Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct的标准七个基准测试集上，HindSearch达到39.4%的平均EM（精确匹配得分），优于现有的搜索强化学习基线。消融实验显示，移除评判模型对黄金答案的访问权限后，大部分性能提升消失，证明后见之明是改进的核心来源。

Conclusion: HindSearch通过引入后见之明的自蒸馏机制，有效利用了失败轨迹中蕴含的丰富信息，显著提升了搜索增强语言模型智能体的训练效果。该工作强调了在强化学习训练中利用失败信息的重要性。

Abstract: Search-augmented LM agents are typically trained with a binary exact-match reward, which throws away most of what a failed trajectory tells us about why it failed. We introduce HindSearch, a hindsight self-distillation procedure for GRPO: after each rollout, a frozen judge writes a short critique of every failed trajectory using the gold answer, and the critique supplies an auxiliary on-policy distillation signal on the student's search actions. On the standard seven-benchmark suite with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, HindSearch reaches 39.4% average EM, outperforming prior search-RL baselines. Removing the judge's access to the gold answer erases most of the gain, isolating hindsight as the source of the improvement.

</details>


### [191] [Latent-Regime Bias Auditing for Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01599)
*Arthur Chagas,Pedro Bento,Yan Aquino,Arthur Buzelin,Wagner Meira,Cristiano Arbex Valle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关的波动率预测审计框架，通过学习市场状态窗口的时间序列表示并聚类为潜在市场机制，评估波动率预测在不同机制下的条件可靠性，而非仅依赖总体平均精度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚合精度指标（如RMSE、MAE）可能掩盖在特定市场机制下预测失效的问题，而这些条件性失败对风险管理至关重要。

Method: 学习市场状态窗口的时间序列表示，仅使用训练信息聚类为潜在机制，在样本外分配机制，并将聚合预测行为与机制条件偏差、尾部低估和低估敏感的经济损失进行比较。

Result: 在加密货币和ETF资产的日度波动率预测上，具有竞争性聚合精度的模型仍可能表现出显著的机制特定偏差和严重的尾部低估。

Conclusion: 波动率预测评估不应只关注平均误差，还应识别预测在哪些市场机制下会条件性失效，从而将评估范式从"平均最准确"转向"识别模型在何处失效"。

Abstract: Volatility forecasts are commonly evaluated with aggregate accuracy metrics such as RMSE and MAE, but these metrics can hide conditional failures that matter for risk management. This paper proposes a model-agnostic audit framework for evaluating whether volatility forecasts remain reliable across latent market regimes. We learn time-series representations of market-state windows, cluster them into regimes using only training information, assign regimes out of sample, and compare aggregate forecast behavior with regime-conditional bias, tail-underprediction, and underprediction-sensitive economic losses. Applied to daily volatility forecasting across cryptocurrency and ETF assets, the audit shows that models with competitive aggregate accuracy can still exhibit substantial regime-specific bias and severe tail underprediction. The results suggest that volatility forecasting should be evaluated not only by average error, but also by where and how forecasts become unreliable. Our framework shifts forecast evaluation from asking which model is most accurate on average to identifying the market regimes in which apparently accurate forecasts fail conditionally. Reproducibility: https://github.com/arthurchagas1/Latent-Regime-Bias-Auditing-for-Volatility-Forecasting

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00001)
*Peter David Fagan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE)框架重新审视莱布尼茨磨坊、图灵测试和塞尔中文房间等经典思想实验，提出将外在任务表现（W_causal,T）与内在操作意识（κ_T）分离分析，指出非压缩查找系统与紧凑生成系统在行为上可相当但内部组织效率迥异，为AI安全分析提供新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的心灵哲学与AI争论（如中文房间、图灵测试）长期混淆了外在工作表现与内在结构组织之间的关系，缺乏一个统一的量化框架来区分二者，因此需要一种新的分析工具来重新审视这些经典问题。

Method: 提出Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE)框架，在符号化玩具设定中形式化两个度量指标：任务表现（W_causal,T）衡量外在行为成功度，操作意识（κ_T）衡量内部结构支持行为的效率；通过对比非压缩查找系统与紧凑生成系统来验证该框架。

Result: 非压缩查找系统与紧凑生成系统在行为表现上可达到可比水平，但前者依赖不断膨胀的未复用映射存储，后者则复用紧凑内部结构，二者在κ_T上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 将外在表现与维持表现的内部组织分离是理解经典"理解"争论的关键，这种区分对后续AI安全分析具有重要意义。

Abstract: This research note revisits Leibniz's mill, Turing's imitation game, and Searle's Chinese Room through the Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE) framework. It formalises a toy symbolic setting in which successful behaviour is measured by task performance ($W_{causal,T}$), while the efficiency with which preserved internal structure supports that behaviour is measured by operational consciousness ($κ_T$). Within this setup, an uncompressed lookup system and a compact generative system can in principle achieve comparable behavioural success, yet diverge sharply in $κ_T$: the former relies on an expanding standing store of unreused mappings, whereas the latter reuses compact internal structure. The note therefore reframes classic disputes about understanding by separating outward performance from the organisation that sustains it, and motivates why this distinction may matter for later AI-safety analysis.

</details>


### [193] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFOAM是一个自进化的LLM智能体，基于自然语言指令自动创建、评估、运行和进化OpenFOAM仿真，通过7阶段进化循环和三种防退化机制，降低CFD使用门槛并加速快速原型开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用开源CFD求解器（如OpenFOAM）需要大量专业知识和技能，且配置文件设置耗时耗力，作者希望降低CFD使用门槛，让非专业用户也能通过自然语言指令进行流体仿真。

Method: 基于Qwen-coder 2.5-14B进行预训练，并在252条文本提示（涵盖7个OpenFOAM求解器、13个参数化网格模板和y plus感知数值策略）上进行微调。核心是包含7个阶段的进化循环，并采用三种互补的抗退化机制：RAG增强重试上下文、外科手术式字典级修补、提示多样性改写。

Result: AutoFOAM能够从自然语言指令自动完成OpenFOAM仿真的创建、评估、运行和进化全过程，有效防止模型在重复自训练下退化，加速了CFD工作流的快速原型开发。

Conclusion: AutoFOAM将生成式AI与严谨的流体仿真相结合，降低了CFD的专业门槛，实现了高级CFD工作流的民主化，为工程领域的快速原型开发提供了新范式。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [194] [Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00006)
*Md. Samiul Islam,Iqbal H. Sarker,Chadni Islam,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种检索增强生成方法，用于缓解大型语言模型在中小企业环境中生成内容的幻觉和错误信息问题，并在多个主流LLM（LLaMA、Mistral、Qwen）上进行实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业在采用LLM辅助问答和决策时，模型生成的幻觉内容可能成为错误信息来源，降低用户对模型可靠性和可信度的信任。因此需要有效方法缓解这一问题。

Method: 提出VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种RAG建模方法，将外部知识源引入LLM生成过程；在多种先进LLM（LLaMA、Mistral、Qwen）上进行实验，从有用响应生成、幻觉风险、上下文相关性及人工解读等维度进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，RAG增强的LLM在响应质量上显著提升，能够有效减少幻觉和错误信息，从而支持中小企业环境中更可靠、可信和上下文感知的决策。

Conclusion: VectorRAG和GraphRAG方法在中小企业场景中能有效缓解LLM幻觉问题，增强决策支持系统的可信度和可靠性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), a part of artificial intelligence (AI), are increasingly being adopted by Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) to enhance question-answering capabilities and support business decision-making processes. However, hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs can serve as a source of misinformation, reducing user confidence in their reliability and trustworthiness within SMEs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising approach to address this challenge by incorporating external knowledge sources into the modeling process. In this paper, we present VectorRAG and GraphRAG modeling approaches to mitigate hallucinations and misinformation risks and evaluate their effectiveness in SME environments. Our experimental evaluation is conducted on multiple state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA, Mistral, and Qwen, to assess performance in terms of useful response generation, risk of hallucination, contextual relevance, as well as human-interpretation. The results demonstrate that RAG-enhanced LLMs can significantly improve response quality by reducing hallucinations and misinformation, thereby supporting more reliable, trustworthy, and context-aware decision-making in SME environments.

</details>


### [195] [Energy Efficiency of Locally Deployed LLMs: A Preliminary Quantitative GPU Power Benchmark on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00008)
*Philipp M. Zähl,Anika Hennig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在单张消费级GPU（RTX 4060Ti 16GB）上对9个开源LLM（1B到7B参数）进行了硬件级别的能耗基准测试，发现模型架构和量化策略比参数数量更影响能效，其中gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b能耗最低（0.56和0.65 J/token），而7B-Mistral能耗最高达最节能模型的4.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署LLM因隐私需求和本地推理而日益普及，但消费级硬件上的能耗成本缺乏充分表征，现有基准主要关注准确率而忽视能效指标。

Method: 在单张RTX 4060Ti 16GB GPU上，使用Ollama推理引擎执行9个开源LLM（1B-7B参数），通过nvidia-smi以2Hz采样GPU功耗，评估平均/峰值功耗、每提示总能耗（J/prompt）、每输出token能耗（J/token）及吞吐量（tok/s）。

Result: gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b能效最高（0.56 J/token和0.65 J/token）且吞吐量最高（>170 tok/s）；7B-Mistral每token能耗是最优模型的4.4倍；qwen3.5:2b因扩展内部推理导致每提示能耗异常偏高。

Conclusion: 模型架构和量化策略是驱动能效的关键因素而非单纯参数数量；能效度量需要区分token生成模式（内部推理与直接输出），以更准确地评估模型实际能耗。

Abstract: The local deployment of large language models (LLMs) is gaining traction due to privacy concerns and the desire for on-premise inference. However, the energy costs on consumer hardware remain poorly characterized, as most benchmarks focus solely on accuracy. This paper presents a reproducible, hardware-level energy benchmark of nine open-source LLMs (1B to 7B parameters) executed on a single consumer GPU (RTX 4060Ti 16GB). Using the Ollama inference engine, GPU power draw was sampled at 2Hz via nvidia-smi across a fixed prompt set. We evaluate mean/peak power, total energy per prompt (J/prompt), energy per output token (J/token), and throughput (tok/s). Our findings suggest that factors beyond raw parameter count, including model architecture and quantization strategy, drive energy efficiency. Specifically, gemma3:1b and llama3.2:1b achieve the lowest energy cost (0.56 J/token and 0.65 J/token) and the highest throughput (>170 tok/s). In contrast, the 7B-Mistral model consumes up to 4.4x more energy per token than the most efficient model. Notably, qwen3.5:2b exhibits anomalously high per-prompt energy due to extended internal reasoning, highlighting the need to distinguish between token generation modes in efficiency metrics.

</details>


### [196] [CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00014)
*Qihua Pan,Zhenheng Tang,Peijie Dong,Xiang Liu,Huacan Wang,Bo Li,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoT-Core框架，通过零样本链式思维推理轨迹将问题映射到潜在空间，按内在逻辑等价性而非表面文本相似度进行核心问题选择，从而在无需历史训练数据的情况下大幅降低LLM评估成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估在持续开发过程中计算开销巨大，现有核心集选择方法要么依赖大量历史日志遭遇"冷启动"瓶颈（如项目反应理论），要么仅基于表面词汇相似度而忽略任务背后的推理逻辑流形。

Method: CoT-Core框架：利用LLM展开零样本链式思维推理轨迹，将推理路径投影到潜在空间，从而按内在逻辑等价性对问题进行聚类，实现免训练的核心问题选择。

Result: 在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA上的广泛实验表明，CoT-Core大幅降低评估成本的同时保持高保真度分数估计，并揭示了推理感知剪枝的边界条件——其有效性受任务复杂度内在门控。

Conclusion: CoT-Core通过推理感知的核心问题选择有效缓解了LLM评估的计算瓶颈，但其效果取决于任务复杂度，为推理感知剪枝方法的适用边界提供了重要洞察。

Abstract: Evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational overhead during continuous development processes. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically disparate questions can share equivalent underlying logic, CoT-Core prompts LLMs to unroll zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories. Projecting these paths into a latent space effectively clusters questions by intrinsic logical equivalence rather than superficial text similarity. Extensive experiments on GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, and GPQA demonstrate that CoT-Core drastically reduces evaluation costs while maintaining high-fidelity score estimation, and delineate the boundary conditions of reasoning-aware pruning, revealing that its efficacy is intrinsically gated by task complexity.

</details>


### [197] [Optimization and Constraint Modeling using LLMs with a Retrieval Augmented Generation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00015)
*Prateek Roy,Akash Singirikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的管道，利用自建的500个合成优化问题数据集（基于Text2Zinc种子描述和LLM生成的专业角色）来提升大语言模型的优化建模性能。在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型上，NL4OPT准确率从40%提升至72%，MAMO Easy从40%提升至56%，MAMO Complex从32%提升至56%。该方法为微调提供了有效的低成本替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化建模和约束建模是复杂且需要深度领域专业知识的问题，现有大语言模型在组合优化场景下经常产生结构不一致或不完整的优化公式，需要探索无需昂贵微调即可提升LLM优化建模能力的方法。

Method: 构建500个合成优化问题（使用Text2Zinc种子描述和LLM创建的专业角色，以JSON格式指定并配有验证过的Python求解器脚本），编码到Chroma向量数据库。推理时检索语义相似问题作为上下文引导，供LangChain LLM代理使用。在三个基准测试集上使用Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型评估。

Result: NL4OPT准确率从40%提升至72%，MAMO Easy从40%提升至56%，MAMO Complex从32%提升至56%。语义验证的合成示例大幅提升了求解准确性和结构完整性。

Conclusion: 合成数据集生成与检索增强的结合为微调提供了有效替代方案，领域特定合成语料库配合RAG可成为在现实决策支持场景中部署LLM优化工具的实际路径，无需昂贵的模型重训练。

Abstract: Both optimization modeling and constraint modeling are non-trivial problems requiring deep domain expertise and proficiency in modeling formalism languages. Despite their importance across logistics, healthcare, and supply chain management, current large language models regularly produce structurally inconsistent or incomplete optimization formulations, particularly in combinatorial settings. This paper evaluates whether a Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline built on a curated synthetic dataset can meaningfully improve LLM optimization modeling performance. A total of 500 optimization problems were synthesized using seed descriptions from the Text2Zinc dataset and professional personas created using an LLM, specified in JSON and associated with validated Python solver scripts. These problems were encoded in a Chroma vector database. For each inference problem, semantically similar problems were retrieved and used as contextual guidance for a LangChain LLM agent. Three benchmark testbeds were used to evaluate the proposed pipeline under the Qwen 3 30B Instruct model. Accuracy rose from 40% to 72% on NL4OPT, 40% to 56% on MAMO Easy, and 32% to 56% on MAMO Complex. The use of semantically validated synthetic examples greatly improves both solution accuracy and structure. The combination of synthetic dataset generation with retrieval augmentation provides an effective alternative to fine-tuning, suggesting that domain-specific synthetic corpora paired with retrieval augmentation can serve as a practical pathway for deploying LLM-based optimization tools in real-world decision-support contexts without costly model retraining.

</details>


### [198] [Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)
*Mohammad Asadolahi,Amir Amini,Samira Talebi,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于外部记忆的LLM智能体自我改进过程中，因使用LLM自我评分替代真实标签而产生的"回音差距"（Echo Gap）失败模式，并提出"错误独立性假设"（EIA）作为纠正分数膨胀的必要条件，以及无答案去膨胀算法LUCID来提升智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆型LLM智能体通过存储过往经验并评分来改进后续行为，但部署时无法获得真实标签，只能用LLM自我评估替代，导致错误记忆获得虚高评分并被反复重用，错误通过记忆不断累积而非抵消。现有方法无法识别哪些记忆被高估，因为验证者的错误和原始自我评分偏差相关。

Method: 形式化定义"错误独立性假设"（EIA），证明其为纠正分数膨胀的必要条件；证明膨胀不仅在按存储分数排序检索时出现，在按相似度检索时同样存在；提出无答案去膨胀算法LUCID，在无需真实答案的情况下对记忆评分进行去偏处理。

Result: 在BIRD文本转SQL基准上，LUCID算法将执行准确率提升至56.9%，优于Memento风格自评分智能体（54.0%，平均提升+2.9个百分点）以及同架构的无记忆智能体（52.4%）。

Conclusion: 记忆评分必须同时追踪真实性与去相关于记忆偏见才能有效纠正分数膨胀，EIA是纠偏的必要条件而非仅仅是对好的验证器的描述；通过记忆分数偏差的量化与去膨胀，可以一致性地提升记忆型智能体的性能。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents increasingly learn from experience without updating any weights. Each episode is stored in an external memory, scored, and retrieved for similar future tasks to shape later behavior. Viewed through a reward lens, the stored score is a proxy reward for an implicit, non-parametric policy. Each retrieved episode then becomes a policy-improvement step whose reliability hinges on how that score is produced. In deployment, ground-truth labels are unavailable, so the stored reward is at best an LLM assessment. This substitution creates a failure mode, the *Echo Gap*, across the memory-based self-improving agents and model families studied. Incorrect episodes receive inflated rewards; thus, the agent preferentially reuses the very mistakes it has most confident in. Because the error compounds through memory rather than averaging out and the confirming judge's errors remain correlated with the original self-grading bias, so it cannot identify which memories are overvalued. The missing property is formalized as the *Error-Independence Assumption* (EIA), which we prove is a *necessary* condition for correcting the inflation, not merely a description of a good verifier: a usable signal must track truth *and* decorrelate its error from the memory bias, and the recoverable payoff is a closed-form function of exactly those two quantities. We further show the inflation compounds not only when retrieval ranks by the stored score but also under plain similarity retrieval which is the regime the deployed agent uses. Finally, the answer-free de-inflation algorithm LUCID delivers a consistent end-to-end gain on the BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark. It raises execution accuracy to $56.9\%$, above both a Memento-style self-graded agent ($54.0\%$, a $+2.9$-point mean gain across seeds) and a memory-less agent of identical architecture ($52.4\%$).

</details>


### [199] [Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00026)
*Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Ziwen Wang,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JouleShare框架，通过离线测量建立请求级能量分配的Shapley ground truth，并训练轻量级校准模型JCalib在线上预测公平能量归属，揭示token比例分配在批处理场景下与公平分配存在显著偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量LLM推理服务虽然提升了吞吐量，但GPU功率遥测是聚合性的，无法直接获得请求级别的能耗归属。现有基准只提供模型级、阶段级或token级能耗，缺乏请求级的实测ground truth，导致实践中使用的能耗分配规则与公平分配之间的偏差程度一直未知。

Method: 提出JouleShare双组件框架：(1) 离线测试平台通过vLLM重放请求子集，整合GPU功率遥测，计算每个请求的精确Shapley能量作为ground truth；(2) JCalib轻量级校准模型，从廉价请求特征学习预测Shapley份额，用于在线服务时的能量归属。

Result: 在16组模型/工作负载实验中，token比例分配与精确Shapley值的平均归一化L1误差在静态批处理下为0.440，连续批处理下为0.458，且在三种数据中心GPU上均可复现。JCalib将误差分别降低至0.116和0.177，优于不可在线获取的独立测量基线，同时保持批量能量效率。采样Shapley将测量参考扩展到更大组规模，误差仍然存在，单一离线校准仍是最准确的可用部署规则。

Conclusion: token归属不能作为批处理执行下边际能量的可靠代理，实测的Shapley ground truth可以校准低成本请求特征，实现更公平的能耗归属。

Abstract: Batched LLM serving improves throughput but complicates energy accounting. GPU power telemetry is aggregate, whereas sustainability reporting, chargeback, and workload analysis often require request-level energy charges. Existing inference-energy benchmarks report model-, phase-, or token-level energy, and recent carbon-accounting work motivates Shapley fairness conceptually. Neither provides measured request-level ground truth, so how far the accounting rules used in practice deviate from a fair allocation has remained unknown. We present JouleShare, an attribution framework with two components. An offline harness establishes this ground truth by replaying request subsets under vLLM with a reproducible protocol, integrating GPU power telemetry, and computing exact Shapley energy for each request. A lightweight calibration model, JCalib, then learns to predict Shapley shares from cheap request features for use at serving time. Across 16 model/workload runs, token-proportional attribution differs from exact Shapley by 0.440 normalized L1 on average under static batching and by 0.458 under continuous batching, a gap that reproduces across three data-center GPUs. JCalib reduces this error to 0.116 under static batching and 0.177 under continuous batching, below even a standalone-measurement baseline that is unavailable online, while preserving exact batch-energy efficiency. Sampled Shapley extends the measured reference to larger group sizes, where the gap persists and a single offline calibration remains the most accurate deployable rule. The results show that token attribution is not a reliable proxy for marginal energy under batched execution, and that measured Shapley ground truth can calibrate low-cost request features toward fairer attribution.

</details>


### [200] [Motif-Mamba: network motif improved mamba for long-range sequence modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00027)
*Chonghe Hao,Yue Sun,Jian Zhang,Yansong Wang,Wangzi Yao,Yunjie Yao,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Motif-Mamba，一种在Mamba基础上引入模体约束低秩循环通路的结构化状态空间模型，在保持线性时间复杂度的同时增强跨维度信息交互，在长序列外推、语言建模和脑机接口解码任务上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba通过选择性状态空间递归实现了线性时间复杂度的长序列建模，但其对角状态转移限制了状态维度间的显式交互，制约了模型表达能力。作者希望在不牺牲线性效率的前提下增强跨维度通信。

Method: 受三节点网络模体动力学启发，Motif-Mamba在Mamba基础上增加一个模体约束的低秩循环通路：先将隐状态投影到紧凑动力学子空间，施加模体引导的维度间交互，再将动力学结果映射回原始状态空间，从而增强跨维度信息交互且保持线性递归结构。

Result: 在长序列外推、语言建模基准和脑机接口解码等实验上均取得了优于Mamba骨干网络的一致改进。

Conclusion: 模体引导的低秩动力学为长程序列建模提供了一种有效的结构先验，能够在保持Mamba线性效率的同时提升模型性能。

Abstract: Efficient long-sequence modeling remains a central challenge for large language models, as self-attention scales quadratically with sequence length. Mamba offers a linear-time alternative through selective state space recurrence, but its predominantly diagonal state transitions restrict explicit interactions among state dimensions. We propose Motif-Mamba, a structured state space model that augments Mamba with a motif-constrained low-rank recurrent pathway. Inspired by the dynamics of three-node network motifs, the proposed pathway projects hidden states into a compact dynamical subspace, imposes motif-guided interactions, and maps the resulting dynamics back to the original state space. This design enhances cross-dimensional communication while preserving the linear-time recurrent structure of Mamba. Experiments on long-sequence extrapolation, language modeling benchmarks, and brain--computer interface decoding show consistent improvements over Mamba backbones, suggesting that motif-guided low-rank dynamics provide an effective structural prior for long-range sequence modeling.

</details>


### [201] [Nova: An End-to-End MLIR Compiler for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00029)
*Adwaid Suresh,Aparna A,Harshini V M,Jona Delcy C A,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Ram Charan Golla,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nova是一个针对深度学习硬件映射的端到端JIT编译器，通过捕获eager执行、融合前后向传播为单一值语义方言，利用解析配置器零搜索时间推导最优执行调度，并结合结构哈希运行时合成细粒度内核。在RTX 3060上，Nova在TF32矩阵乘法上匹配或略超cuBLAS和XLA，模型级吞吐量最高比PyTorch高10.6%，比XLA高4.4%，内存占用降低最多29%，能在PyTorch遭遇OOM的12GB GPU上成功训练1.44亿参数模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型扩展性能严重依赖高级数学运算到物理硬件的映射效率，而现有的eager执行模型缺乏整图可见性和对硬件/内存的细粒度控制，无法原生最大化物理硬件利用率。

Method: Nova设计了三层核心机制：(1) 捕获eager执行并将前向与反向传播统一为单一值语义方言，实现全图级优化；(2) 利用Analytic Configurator基于算术强度确定性推导最优执行调度，将搜索时间降为零；(3) 通过结构哈希运行时直接从计算结构合成细粒度内核。

Result: 在RTX 3060上，Nova在大部分形状的TF32矩阵乘法上匹配或略超cuBLAS和XLA（相对误差<5e-4）；模型级吞吐量比PyTorch提升最高10.6%，比XLA提升4.4%；内存占用较PyTorch降低最高29%，使1.44亿参数模型能在12GB GPU上以17,900 tokens/s训练，而PyTorch在同一硬件上OOM。

Conclusion: Nova证明了通过全图级优化、确定性调度推导和结构驱动的内核合成，可以实现在不牺牲数值精度的前提下显著提升深度学习模型的吞吐量和内存效率，从而在受限的消费级GPU硬件上训练更大规模的模型。
